115年 9月全國賦稅收入初步統計 
一、全國賦稅收入 
（一）本月數 
9 月實徵淨額 4,965 億元，較上年同月增加 813 億元(+19.6%)。 
（二）累計數 
累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 3 兆 5,714 億元，較上年同期增加 7,004 億
元(+24.4%)。 
（三）達成率 
1.中央政府總預算業於 9 月 18 日公布，稅課收入較原預算案提高
6,000 億元，併計地方政府及特種基金後，全國稅課收入較預算案
提高 5,852 億元，本次發布均改依法定數計算預算達成率(實徵淨
額占全年預算數)。 
2.累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額占累計分配預算數 115.0%，占全年預算數
78.8%。 
圖 1 全國賦稅收入及年增率

說明：115 年 5-7 月年增率跳動較大，係因 114 年所得稅結算申報及繳納期限延長至 6 月底所致。

二、主要稅目別變動
（一）9 月實徵淨額與上年同月比較，以營業稅增加 297 億元、營利事業
所得稅增加 254 億元及證券交易稅增加 222 億元較多。
（二）累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額與上年同期比較，以證券交易稅增加 3,077
億元、營利事業所得稅增加 1,853 億元及營業稅增加 1,203 億元較多。
圖 2 主要稅目別變化
(115 年 1-9 月)
1.營利事業所得稅
(1)9 月實徵淨額 2,007 億元，較上年同月增加 254 億元(+14.5%)，主
因暫繳稅款成長。
(2)累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 1 兆 28 億元，較上年同期增加 1,853 億元
(+22.7%)，主因結算申報自繳 、暫繳稅款 俱增及延分期稅款陸續
入帳。
2.綜合所得稅
9 月實徵淨額 490 億元，較上年同月增加 34 億元(+7.4%)，主因扣繳
稅款增加所致；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 8,279 億元，較上年同期增加
790 億元(+10.6%)，主因結算申報自繳增加，以及盈餘分配、薪資所

得等扣繳稅款均呈成長。
3.營業稅
9月實徵淨額1,414億元，較上年同月增加297億元(+26.6%)，累計1至
9月實徵淨額6,230億元，較上年同期增加1,203億元(+23.9%)，均因內
需穩定，加以電子零組件 、資通與視聽產品 等進口代徵稅額增加 ，
惟出口暢旺，適用外銷零稅率營業人申請退稅亦增，抵銷部分增幅。

### Page 3

3
4.貨物稅
(1)9月實徵淨額100億元，較上年同月減少11億元(-10.0%)，主因汽柴
油減徵幅度擴大，惟電動車大量到港推升進口 車稅額，抵銷部分
減幅。
(2)累計1至9月實徵淨額874億元，較上年同期減少207億元(-19.2%)，
除汽柴油降稅因素外 ，亦與新購汽機車及電動車之減免徵退稅增
加有關。
5.證券交易稅
(1)9 月及累計 1 至 9 月上市上櫃股票平均每日成交值為 1 兆 317 億
元、1 兆 1,461 億元，分別較上年同期(6,006 億元、4,451 億元)增
71.8%、1.6 倍。
(2)9 月 證 交 稅 實 徵 淨 額534 億 元， 較 上 年 同 月 增 加 222 億 元
(+71.2%)；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 5,060 億元，較上年同期增加
3,077 億元(+1.6 倍)。
6.關稅
9 月實徵淨額 157 億元，較上年同月增加 3 億元(+1.9%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 1,224 億元，較上年同期增加 80 億元(+7.0%)，均因機械

及電機設備、基本金屬及其製品等進口稅額增加。
7.贈與稅
9 月實徵淨額 23 億元，較上年同月增加 6 億元(+35.7%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 211 億元，較上年同期增加 43 億元(+25.3%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加。
8.土地增值稅
9 月實徵淨額 56 億元，較上年同月增加 12 億元(+26.6%)，主因大額
稅款案件增加；累計 1 至 9 月實徵淨額 504 億元，較上年同期減少
14 億元(-2.8%)，主因房地交易量及大額稅款案件減少。
9.印花稅
9 月實徵淨額 31 億元，較上年同月增加 4 億元(+16.2%)，累計 1 至 9
月實徵淨額 175 億元，較上年同期增加 15 億元(+9.0%)，主因銀行放
款稅額成長。
（三）前 9月主要稅目實徵淨額占分配預算數之比率：
1.大於 100%者有證交稅(272%)、遺產稅(155%)、贈與稅(132%)、印花
稅(130%)、營業稅(116%)、關稅(114%)、契稅(111%)、房屋稅(106%)、

綜所稅(101%)、牌照稅(101%)及營所稅(101%)等。
2.低於 100%者有貨物稅(98%)、菸酒稅(97%)、土增稅(85%)、地價稅
(84%)及特銷稅(57%)等。

### Page 4

4
三、各級政府賦稅收入概況
(一)中央政府
累計 1 至 9 月中央政府賦稅收入 (不含中央統籌分配稅款 )實徵淨 額
2 兆 3,603 億元(占分配預算數 119.3%)，較上年同期增加 2,553 億元
(+12.1%)，雖新版財政收支劃分法 開始施行，營業稅劃歸中央政府 比
率大幅調降，減少 2,748 億元(-90.7%)，惟證交稅、所得稅分別增加 3,077
億元、2,230 億元，互抵後呈現上升。
(二)地方政府
累計 1 至 9 月地方政府賦稅收
入(含中央統籌分配稅款 )實徵
淨額 1 兆 898 億元(占分配預
算數 109.4%)，較上年同期增
加 4,527 億元(+71.0%)，主因
納入中央統籌分配 之營業稅
增加 4,030 億元(+2.1 倍)所致。
(三)中央特種基金
累計 1 至 9 月撥入中央特種基金之健康福利捐實徵淨額 195 億元，較
上年同期增加 2 億元(+0.8%)；房地合一課徵所得稅撥入住宅基金 135
億元，較上年同期減少 7 億元(-4.7%)；撥入長照基金 883 億元(其中 541

億元來自房地合一課徵所得稅、195 億元來自菸稅，146 億元來自遺贈
稅)，較上年同期增加 8 億元(+0.9%)。

足許の経済・金融状況と注目ポイント
1
（注）予想はLSEG、ブルームバーグが取りまとめた予想コンセンサス
（出所）LSEG、ブルームバーグより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
2026. 10.27
先週（10/19～10/26）発表の主な経済指標
米生産活動は堅調。AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子
製品や機械などがけん引。一方、データセンター建設に伴う需要
増加が、他産業の生産や建設活動を圧迫する兆候も
コア消費者物価は再び2％超え。特殊要因を除くサービス価格
も加速し、人件費転嫁の兆し。連合は2027年春闘で6％中心
を要求。中小企業の余力に課題も、物価上振れに注意
注目点② サービス物価の加速で重み増す2027年春闘戦線注目点①AI投資の恩恵拡大、副作用もじわり顕在化
総合PMIは57.6と高水準を維持。新規失業保険申
請件数も19.4万件と低水準で解雇の動きは引き続
き限定的。一方で、住宅着工件数や住宅建設業
者の景況感は悪化、高金利の影響が継続

製造業
非耐久財
一次金属
一般機械
コンピューター・電子製品
家具
（2024年12月比、％）
（年/月）
コメント要旨出典
耐久財製造業は、軍需やデータセンター（DC）増
設の旺盛な需要が、他の製品の生産を圧迫して
いると報告ベージュブック
（2026/10） テクノロジー企業がDC向け電気設備の買い占め
を試みたが、供給業者は他案件への供給を優先
し、これを拒否
機械製造業は、DC向けに鋼材在庫が大量に消
費されるなか、鉄鋼メーカーによる供給制限が他
の建設案件の遅延を招いていると指摘
テキサス州
見通し調査
（2026/9）
製造業では原材料コストが上昇。特に鋼材価格

―AI投資の拡大が関連産業の生産を押し上げる一方、供給制約を通じて他産業の活動を圧迫する兆候も
― 当面はAI投資による成長押し上げ効果が期待される一方、供給制約や価格上昇を通じた負の影響にも注意が必要

― 日本からの距離が相対的に遠い欧州・米国の航空運賃上昇が顕著

14

― 国内物価高や円安などが支えとなり消費単価は同＋6.1％と増加。一方、訪日外客数は中東情勢悪化による減便や航

顧
市場焦點
美國
• 9月ISM服務業PMI降至54.9，雖商業活動與新訂單放緩 ，但就業回升至 50.1、訂單積壓增加 ，
顯示服務需求仍具韌性；另一方面，價格指數升至74.0，創2022年7月以來新高，供應商交貨
速度亦放緩 ，反映燃料 、原物料及供應鏈成本升高 ，景氣未明顯轉弱之際 ，通膨壓力反而重
新升溫；同時全球長債殖利率同步走高 ，AI基建投資延續企業成長動能 ，也提高資本需求 。
整體而言 ，美國經濟尚未明顯轉弱 ，但通膨與長端利率壓力同步升高 ，使金融條件維持偏緊 。
中國
• 彭博指出在政府積極推動開源策略與 AI普及下，中國產業快速發展出現供給過剩 ，產業獲利
能力面臨挑戰，未來三年中國AI產業將可能處於虧損狀態，不過即使模型價格下降，AI使用量
增加將帶動Token需求大幅成長，其中具有企業客戶、專有資料及生態優勢的AI龍頭，將比單

純提供模型服務的公司更具長期競爭力。
黃金
• 歐美國家財政可持續性的疑慮持續發酵 ，加上長期通膨風險升溫 ，宏觀不確定性讓實體黃金
市場逆勢吸金 。本波下跌主要反映去槓桿交易 ，實體黃金的基本面未變 ，有望為金價帶來中
期的支撐。建議投資人逢修正分批進場 ，可透過黃金存摺或是實體黃金 ETF(GLD)，以投資組
合多元化的角度布局。波段投機者，從技術指標來看，可留意4,000美元關卡是否出現支撐。

美國9月ISM服務需求略降、成本壓力升高，景氣韌性與通膨風險並存
• 服務業維持擴張，需求略降但就業回穩：9月ISM服務業指數降至54.9(前月55.4)，連續27個月位於擴張區間；商業活動
指數降至56.5、新訂單降至 59.8，顯示服務需求較前月放緩 ，但就業指數回升至 50.1、重返擴張區間 ，訂單積壓升至
56.6，顯示內需仍具韌性，服務業尚未出現明顯轉弱訊號。
• 成本與供應鏈壓力升高 ，通膨降溫面臨干擾： 價格指數升至 74.0，創2022年7月以來新高 ，且為7個月來第6次高於70；
供應商交貨指數升至53.2，顯示交貨速度放緩，燃料、柴油、鋼材、銅及記憶體等成本上升，加上供應受限，使服務業
投入成本壓力擴大，通膨降溫進程面臨干擾。
• 市場觀察：美國服務業為經濟成長的重要支撐，目前經濟呈現「需求仍強、通膨升溫」的拉鋸格局，雖服務業景氣支撐

美國企業獲利，但成本上升可能推升利率波動，投資上宜偏向美國短中天期投資級債，兼顧較高收益率與利率風險控管；
股票則可聚焦具定價能力、獲利與現金流穩健的優質企業。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.6 資料來源：ISM Services PMI Report， 2026.10.6
ISM服務業指數分項指標變動對比表ISM服務業PMI主要分項指數變化

場

顧
全球利率同步走高、AI投資持續擴張，長端美債壓力仍高
• 全球長債殖利率同步走高，反映利率環境重新定價：美國財政部長貝森特指出，近期美債殖利率攀升與德國、日本等主
要債市同步上行，反映全球資本成本重新定價，而非市場對美國資產失去信心；加上目前尚未出現資金大規模撤離美債
並轉向其他主權債券的現象，因此債市波動較偏向全球利率環境調整，而非金融系統性風險。
• AI投資增加資金需求，支撐較高利率環境：貝森特亦駁斥AI泡沫論，認為微軟、Alphabet及Meta等大型科技企業仍以
實際獲利與現金流支撐持續擴大AI資本支出，不同於過去缺乏基本面的科技泡沫；龐大的資料中心 、電力與半導體投資
需求提高整體資金成本，也成為推升長天期利率的重要因素之一。
• 市場觀察：目前市場經濟近況屬於利率高檔、成長尚穩的情境中，因而投資策略建議配置大型科技股、AI基建供應鏈及

受惠利差環境的金融股具相對優勢；債券配置則以中短天期投資級債優先，避免長天期債券受利率上行壓力侵蝕。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.6

• 觀察美國本土投資能否進一步轉化為出口能力：AI建設帶動半導體與設備進口，加上能源成本上升及關稅政策干擾，推升進

師預期第三季標普500企業獲利年增約29.5%，加上企業獲利預期持續上修，強化投資人對高利率環境下企業基本面的信心。

擴大，且企業治理改革、AI投資及自動化需求仍提供獲利支撐。近期日股AI與半導體族群已明顯走強，顯示市場仍重視企業
獲利成長；中長期建議維持日股正向看法，聚焦金融、AI、機器人、自動化及電力設備等具結構性成長題材的企業。

中國AI產業陷入逐底競爭困境
• 中國AI產業陷入逐底競爭困境 ：彭博指出在政府積極推動開源策略與
AI普及下，中國AI產業快速發展 ，但也逐漸出現供給過剩 ，使產業獲
利能力面臨挑戰 。開源模式雖有助於降低技術門檻 、應用普及 ，但也
使技術優勢難以長期維持 ，加上中國市場長期習慣低價甚至免費的數
位服務，多數AI業者難以透過訂閱模式獲利，轉而依賴利潤較低的API
服務作為主要收入來源 。目前中國已備案大型語言模型 (LLM)數量達
988個，包括DeepSeek、Kimi及智譜AI等新創企業快速崛起，進一步
加劇市場競爭。此外API服務逐漸被視為基礎公用服務，即使AI Agent
帶動詞元(Token)需求快速成長，也未必能同步提升獲利，由於開源模
型可由不同雲端業者部署 ，市場進入門檻降低 ，業者更難建立長期競

爭優勢。
• 後市展望： 考量龐大資本支出與持續價格競爭 ，未來三年中國 AI產業
整體仍可能處於虧損狀態 ，市場將形成「美國閉源、高毛利」與「中
國開源 、低成本 」模式 ，即使模型價格下降 ，AI 使用量增加將帶動
Token需求大幅成長 ，進而推升資料中心 、雲端服務及 AI晶片需求 ，
從投資角度來看，基礎設施供應商(晶片、伺服器、資料中心、雲端)受
惠程度可能高於模型開發商；而具有企業客戶 、專有資料及生態優勢
的AI龍頭，將比單純提供模型服務的公司更具長期競爭力。

中國已備案的LLM數量

人行下調抵押補充貸款利率和房貸貼息等政策出台，有望提振中國經濟
和市場信心
• 人行下調抵押補充貸款利率和房貸貼息等政策出台 ，有望提振中國經濟和市場信心 ：日前中國推出購房貸款貼息政策及下調
PSL(抵押補充貸款)利率，代表穩增長政策進一步加碼 ，有助於提振市場信心與經濟預期 ，此次政策不僅支持房地產市場 ，也
將「六張網」建設、科技創新、民營及小微企業 ，納入重點扶持範圍 ，透過增加再貸款額度 ，與提高支持比例 ，帶動投資、
就業及消費回升。短期A股有望在政策推動下出現築底反彈，其中基建投資、製造業投資、房市銷售及居民消費有機會改善，
同時PSL(抵押補充貸款)降息有助於流動性環境邊際寬鬆 ，降低市場對經濟增長的疑慮 ，後市來看企業獲利與信用環境有望逐
步修復，進一步鞏固A股慢牛格局。投資建議上房貸貼息政策有利房地產、建材、家居等產業鏈，金融業受惠資金成本下降與

信貸回升，食品飲料、零售、家電及服務消費則可望受惠於居民消費意願提升。
資料來源：華金證券，2026.10.5

中國、日本減持美國公債的背後邏輯與差異
• 橋水基金創始人達利歐警告 ，美國公債正面臨中日兩國減持的風險 ，數據顯示這項警告已成事實 。2026年前七個月，中國
美國公債持倉減少660億美元至6,180億美元；同期日本持倉亦減少820億美元至1.1兆美元，扣除價格變動的估值效應後，
兩國實質持倉均大幅下滑。然而，兩國削減美國公債的背後動機並不相同，這將導致未來的減持軌跡出現明顯分化。
• 中國戰略性長期去美元化：1.降低地緣政治風險與轉向資產多元化 ，隨著人民幣在國際貿易結算中使用增加 ，市場流動性提
升，中國對美元儲備依賴度顯著下降。2.美債財政可持續性隱憂，美國政府債務持續攀升，引發對美國長期財政健康疑慮。
3.根據彭博經濟研究模型預測，在漸進式減持情境下，中國的美元儲備(以美債與機構債為主)到2050年可能減少約50%。

• 中國搭配戰術性節奏調整 (短期緩和因素)：儘管長期趨勢向下 ，但目前中美利差顯著 (美中30年期公債利差超過 350個基點)，
在美債殖利率維持高位期間，過快減持意味著實現債券價格下跌的虧損，且可能加劇人民幣升值壓力。因此，中國短中期內
傾向採取「戰略性減持、戰術性放緩」的靈活步調。
美國公債持倉：中國 vs 日本
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.8，美國財政部
中國預計將減少美元儲備

日本受政策利率與本土資本回流驅動
• 與中國的結構性考量不同，日本的減持主要出自週期性因素與匯率干預需求。
• 日本央行貨幣政策轉鷹： 日本央行正加快升息腳步 。隨著日債殖利率上揚 (30年期日債殖利率升至約 4.2%)，美日利差持續
收窄，吸引日本國內機構資金回流本土主權債市場。
• 匯率干預政策：日本為防範日圓過度貶值，日本財務省傾向出售部分海外美債資產以籌集外匯干預資金。若日圓再次面臨壓
力，此類拋售干預可能持續。因此，隨著日本央行逐步走向政策正常化，日本對美債的持倉大概會延續下滑軌跡。
• 小結與投資建議：美國公債作為全球無風險資產的地位正面臨結構性挑戰，中國的「長期去美元化」與日本的「本土利率回
升資金回流」將形成雙重壓力，這意味著美債殖利率短期內難大幅下行，美國財政部發債成本居高不下，進而推高全球資本

的融資成本。投資債券配置建議採取「中短天期＋階梯式配置」策略，長天期美國公債(20年以上)受發債供給過剩及中日兩
國長期減持影響，價格波動較大。建議投資人適度降低長天期美國公債暴險，轉向防禦性較佳的中短天期美國公債或高品質
高評級公司債，以鎖定當前較高殖利率同時控管利率風險。
殖利率差：美元與人民幣 vs 美元與日圓
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.29

黃金市場資金流向分歧，實體投資有望支撐中長期金價表現
• 利率與匯率因素，壓抑金價近期表現：9月以來，美國公債殖利率飆升、美元指數走揚，由於黃金本身不孳息，資金傾向轉
往固定收益市場，疊加美元走強壓抑國際購買意願，金價自8月底高點至今已下修約11%。
• 9月資金流向分歧，實體投資強、期貨市場弱：根據世界黃金協會統計，9月在金價下跌之際，全球實體黃金ETF卻吸引大量
資金流入，流入約100億美元(67噸)。另一方面，COMEX黃金期貨市場投機部位大幅下降 ，避險基金與其他機構投資人的
多頭部位明顯減少，造成期貨市場賣壓增加，進一步推動金價下跌。
• 實體黃金市場逆勢吸金反應投資人的避險需求：歐美國家財政可持續性的疑慮持續發酵，加上美伊衝突未解及AI算力需求強
勁，長期通膨風險升溫，宏觀的不確定性讓實體黃金市場逆勢吸金。從數據上來看，本波下跌主要反映去槓桿交易，實體黃

金的基本面未變 ，有望為金價帶來中期的支撐 。建議投資人逢修正分批進場 ，可透過黃金存摺或是實體黃金 ETF(GLD)，以
投資組合多元化的角度布局。波段投機者，從技術指標來看，可留意4,000美元關卡是否出現支撐。
資料來源：世界黃金協會，2026.10.7。
實體黃金ETF淨流量 費氏係數顯示4,000美元附近或有轉折

3. 基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益 ，投資人申購
前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

2026年 10月 30日 全8頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 9月雇用統計 
失業率は2.4％と前月から0.1％pt低下し、就業拡大が進展

経済調査部 エコノミスト 菊池 慈陽

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 9月の完全失業率（季節調整値）は 2.4％と、2カ月ぶりに低下（前月差▲
0.1％pt）し、7月の水準に戻った。失業者数は「自発的な離職」を中心に 2カ月ぶり
に減少（同▲10万人）し、就業者数は2カ月連続で増加（同＋34万人）した。労働参
加と就業拡大が進展し、雇用環境は前月から改善したとみられる。 
◼ 2026年9月の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は1.19倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と3カ月ぶり
に上昇した。新規求職申込件数が大きく減少したことで、新規求人倍率は2.19倍（同
＋0.13pt）と上昇した。 
◼ 先行きの雇用環境は堅調に推移しよう。労働供給が中長期的に減少していく可能性が
高いこともあり、企業は高水準の賃上げなど、人材確保に向けた積極的な取り組みを
続けている。ただし、雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない。中東情勢をめぐる不確実
性は依然として高く、長期化する原油高が企業収益を悪化させ、幅広い産業で労働需
要を下押しする恐れがある。

9月の完全失業率：2.4％と前月から低下、「自発的な離職」による失業者が大きく減少 
2026年 9月の完全失業率（季節調整値）は2.4％と、2カ月ぶりに低下（前月差▲0.1％pt）
し、7月の水準に戻った（図表2左上） 。失業者数は2カ月ぶりに減少（同▲10万人）し、就業
者数は 2カ月連続で増加（同＋34万人）した。労働参加と就業拡大が進展し、雇用環境は前月
から改善したとみられる。 
失業者数を求職理由別に見ると、「自発的な離職」 （前月差▲13万人）が大きく減少し、全体
を押し下げた。「勤め先や事業の都合による離職」（同▲1万人）は小幅に減少した。他方、「定
年又は雇用契約の満了」（同＋1万人）と「新たに求職」（同＋1万人）は小幅に増加した（巻末
の雇用概況①下段左）。 
就業者数の前月からの変化を業種別に見ると、「製造業」と「医療，福祉」が大きく増加し、
全体を押し上げた。「卸売業，小売業」と「情報通信業」も増加した。他方、「建設業」やその
他の非製造業は減少した。（図表2左下）。 
雇用者数（役員を除く）を雇用形態別に見ると、正規雇用者は前月から 22万人増加した。
2026年春頃から増加傾向にあり、ピークだった2025年 11月の水準を上回った。非正規雇用者
は 5カ月ぶりに増加（前月差＋21万人）した（図表 2右下）。 
図表 2：就業者数・完全失業率（左上） 、非労働力人口・労働参加率（右上） 、

9月の新規求人倍率：求職が大きく減少した一方で求人は増加し、前月から上昇 
2026年 9月の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は 1.19倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と 3カ月ぶりに上
昇した。新規求人倍率は2.19倍（同＋0.13pt）と上昇した（図表3左）。 
求人側の動きを見ると、有効求人数は前月から横ばいだった一方、新規求人数（前月比＋
1.5％）は 2カ月ぶりに増加した（図表 4左） 。求職側では、有効求職者数（同▲1.4％）、新規
求職申込件数（同▲4.4％）はいずれも 3カ月ぶりに減少した。求職側は 2025年春頃から持ち
直しの動きが見られており、求人側も2026年春頃から増加傾向にある。 
雇用形態別に見ると、正社員の有効求人倍率（季節調整値）は1.01倍（前月差＋0.01pt）と
3カ月ぶりに上昇した。新規求人倍率も 1.79倍（同＋0.11pt）と 3カ月ぶりに上昇した（図表
3右）。

先行き：堅調な推移を見込むも、中東情勢など雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない 
先行きの雇用環境は、堅調に推移することを見込んでいる。労働供給が中長期的に減少して
いく可能性が高いこともあり、企業は高水準の賃上げなど1、人材確保に向けた積極的な取り組
みを続けている。 
日本銀行「全国企業短期経済観測調査」 （日銀短観）の2026年 9月調査では、雇用人員判断
DI（全規模全産業、 「過剰」－「不足」 ）は「最近」が▲38％ptと 6月調査から 1％pt低下し
（人手不足感が強まり）、「先行き」は▲41％ptと更なる低下が予想されている（巻末の雇用概
況①上段右）。特に、中小企業（全産業）は「最近」が▲42％ptと6月調査から3％pt低下し、
「先行き」は▲45％ptだった。中小企業を中心に、人手不足感が強まっているとみられる。 
株式会社ナウキャストによる「HRog賃金 Now」の求人数指数を見ると、正社員とパート・ア
ルバイトのいずれも 2026年春頃から上昇傾向にある（巻末の雇用概況③下段左）。人手不足感
の強まりを受けて、今後も労働需要は堅調に推移するとみられる。 
他方、トランプ米政権による高関税政策（トランプ関税）1F
2や、長期化する日中関係の冷え込
みは、引き続き労働需要の下押し要因となるだろう。 
また、雇用環境の悪化リスクは小さくない。中東情勢は依然として不確実性が高い状況が続
いており、とりわけ注意が必要だ。原油等の供給網の正常化は当面見込みにくく、輸入原油価
格が高止まりしている。原油高は原材料費や物流費などの上昇を通じて企業収益を悪化させ3、
幅広い産業で労働需要を下押しする可能性がある。

1 日本労働組合総連合会（連合）が公表した第 7回（最終）回答集計結果では、定期昇給相当込みの賃上げ率
（加重平均）が企業規模計で 5.01％と、前年（5.25％）をやや下回るものの、高水準を維持している。300人
未満の中小組合では 4.69％と前年（4.65％）をわずかながら上回り、中小企業の積極的な姿勢が目立った。詳
細は、日本労働組合総連合会（連合）「3年連続の 5％超え！～2026春季生活闘争 第7回（最終）回答集計結
果について～」（2026年7月3日）を参照。 
2 トランプ米政権は通商法 122条に基づく措置が失効する 7月 24日に新たな関税措置を発動した（日本経済新
聞 電子版「トランプ政権が新関税発動、日本 12.5％ 中間選挙へ看板政策継続」（2026年7月 24日））。 
3 原油高による物価の上昇については、神田慶司・畑中宏仁・中村華奈子・横田凱「日本経済見通し：2026年
8月」（大和総研レポート、2026年8月 24日）を参照。

「買進」 ，目標價: $165

報告日期：2026.10.28 研 究 員：王穎

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 基於 eMMC 及 NOR 強勁需求，然漲價力道有限，調降為「逢低買進」投資評等，目標價
120 元(4.1X 2027(F)PER)潛在上漲空間 16.5%。

2026/10/27 收盤價： 103.00 ➢ 26Q3 營收 242.37 億，QoQ+26.7%，YoY+195.1%，毛

率已高出貨量於新增產能擴出前成長有限。因此兆豐預估

74.6%，EPS 6.93元。

營 運 分 析

旺宏為全球 NOR Flash領導廠商 
旺宏是全球領先的 NOR Flash 製造商，提供涵蓋廣泛規格和容量的ROM唯
讀記憶體、NOR型及 NAND型快閃記憶體解決方案。目前公司的產能包括8
吋晶圓產能為每月 45-48 千片，12 吋晶圓產能為每月 20 千片，而 ROM 主
要透過外購再進行加工。公司還提供ROM、NOR型快閃記憶體及 NAND型
快閃記憶體的解決方案，並從事次微米邏輯製程、高壓 CMOS 及 BCD製程
的晶圓代工服務，包括嵌入式非揮發性記憶體的 BCD 及邏輯製程。公司的
廠房位於新竹科學園區，包含生產8吋晶圓的晶圓二廠、12吋晶圓的晶圓五
廠，以及產品測試廠。26Q3產品組合：NAND 47%、NOR 46%、ROM 5%、
FBG 2%。

個股報告 
圖三、26Q4產品漲價有利毛利提升 
旺宏各季營收及獲利走勢預估(單位：百萬)

資料來源：旺宏；兆豐投顧預估

NOR Flash 需求穩步上升，車用與伺服器市場成為成長主力 
整體 NOR Flash 產品將持續成長，特別是在車用和伺服器應用方面。隨著汽
車電子化和自動駕駛技術的發展，汽車行業對高可靠性、耐高溫的半導體需
求不斷增加，有利 NOR Flash 在車用市場用量持續增加。此外，伺服器市場
的需求也隨著數據中心的擴展而逐步上升。NOR Flash的快速響應和高可靠
性特性使其非常適合用於伺服器中的關鍵啟動代碼和固件應用。預期旺宏車
規 eMMC 預期於 2027 年開始小量出貨，2028 年放量，增添公司營運動能。

eMMC需求相對強勁，成獲利回溫關鍵 
展望 2026-2027 年，受惠原廠減產趨勢導致 eMMC 需求強勁，車規 eMMC
預期於 2027 年開始小量出貨，2028 年放量，增添公司營運動能，且 NOR 
FLASH 受惠 AI 帶動，需求強勁然後續價格漲幅有限，因應 NAND 及 NOR 
FLASH客戶強勁需求，預期 27H2 年新產能開出，將再增強公司擴力表現。
因此兆豐預估 2026/2027 營收 818.97/1229.03 億元，YoY+183.6/50.1%，
毛利率 66.7/74.1%，稅後淨利 342.1/582.8 億元，EPS 為 17.25/29.39 元。

記憶體持續供不應求，然漲價力道放緩，調降為「逢低買進」投資評等 
旺宏目前位於 3.5X 27(F)PER)，基於 eMMC 及 NOR強勁需求，然漲價力道
有限，調降為「逢低買進」投資評等，目標價 120 元(4.1 x 2 7(F)PER)，潛
在上漲空間 16.5%。

長線來看，車用與伺
服器應用成為 NOR 
Flash成長動能 
漲價力道有限，調降
為「逢低買進」投資
評等,目標價 120元。 
長期看好 NOR 及
eMMC 產品線持續成
長

### Page 1

MediaTek

TPU wins on track for steep ramp through 
’28 joined by further CSP/AI lab potential

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 6,200 TWD | Price: 3,915 TWD

3Q26 beat on consumer builds; 4Q26 lifted by TPU ramp 
3Q26 sales were +10% QoQ on its flagship mobile SoC launch and customers’ pre-builds 
ahead of its chipset price hike on rising manufacturing cost. On higher sales and better 
mix, GM/OpM edged up to 46%/16%, taking 3Q26 EPS to NT$17.5 above BofAe/street. 
4Q26 sales was guided +25-32% QoQ, in line with BofA preview, as steep TPUv8t ramp 
now tracking to US$2.5bn (vs. US$2bn guide before) partially balanced by soft demand 
elsewhere (-15-22% QoQ vs. 5-year seasonal +1%) following the early pull-in. Despite 
TPUv8t growing to ~35% of its sales at lower margins, GMs is guided resiliently at 45% 
in 4Q26, implying better margins for its merchant business at 47%. Fast sales scale-up 
and stable GMs should lift operating leverage with OpMs guided for 19% at midpoint. 
ASIC pipeline spanning across CSPs and frontier AI labs 
Mediatek sees TPUv8t ramping to US$16bn+ revenue in ’27, lifting OpM to 20%+, with 
upside if it could secure more capacity. The follow on TPUv9 is tracking for a meaningful 
production potential in ’28. Importantly, it confirmed a 2nd ASIC customer win in design 
now and production contribution set to kick off in ’28. We believe this new project would 
require Mediatek’s component sourcing and supply chain management at a much higher 
dollar content. This along with potential Bytedance win could add upside to our base 
case for its data center revenue of US$19bn/US$53bn in '27/'28 with xAI ASIC revenue 
joined from 2H28 to supplement Google TPU upgrades.  
AI project diversification supports earnings and valuation 
As ASIC business inflects, we lift our 2026 EPS to NT$74.13 and keep ‘27/28 EPS at 
NT$168.93/404.32. Keep our Buy rating and NT$6,200 PO on 24x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(implied 30x ASIC EPS + 10x merchant EPS), backed by sales/EPS 90%/134% CAGR. 
Growing AI project wins should alleviate the concerns on customer concentration and 
TPU competition debate with share downside protected by 23x/10x ‘27/28 straight P/E.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$6,200 is based on 24x 2H27-1H28 P/E. which is towards the higher end 
of its historical P/E range of 8-25x, as we believe the company's broadening ASIC 
pipeline could drive an earnings upgrade cycle.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

Document 172: 442只翻倍基“全军覆没”，基金经理在反思什么？

Source PDF: all report pdf/442只翻倍基“全军覆没”，基金经理在反思什么？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

442 只翻倍基年内收益中位数已跌⾄ 1.6% ，逾 200 只转负，峰值 189% 的财通多策略福鑫缩
⽔⾄ 12% 。基⾦经理集体反思：过度聚焦产业逻辑，忽视筹码拥挤度与流动性⻛险，个股
分散却⾏业⾼度集中。调整后分歧显现 —— 部分经理逢低加仓 AI 算⼒，另⼀些则转向
CXO 、煤炭、内需⻰头以平衡⻛险敞⼝。
摘要：
市场震荡之际，公募基⾦三季报已披露完毕。美联储重启加息、美债收益率⾼位、海外流动性
偏紧、中东战事推升油价等变量在三季报中被反复提及。它们压缩了⾼估值成⻓股的远期定价
空间，也让基⾦经理不得不重新回答⼀个问题：为了 AI （⼈⼯智能）未来的增⻓，当下⽀付多少
价格合适？
主动权益基⾦是本轮调整中回撤最深的⼀类。《财经》根据 Wind （万得）数据，按⼆级投资分类
统计，截⾄ 10 ⽉ 30 ⽇，主动权益类基⾦年内平均回报为 -12% ，最⼤回撤平均值为 -30% ， 7671
只收益为负，占 84% 。同期全市场基⾦年内平均回报为 -7% ，最⼤回撤平均值为 -18% 。

上涨时最陡的⼀批基⾦，回落也最深。年内最⼤涨幅超过 100% 的主动权益基⾦有 442 只。截⾄
10 ⽉ 30 ⽇，这批基⾦的当前年内回报中位数已回落⾄ 1.6% ，平均回撤 51% ，其中收益率最⾼的
为易⽅达供给改⾰，收益率为 96% ，也就是说年内已⽆翻倍基。超过 200 只转为负收益，占⽐近
五成。峰值收益最⾼的基⾦财通多策略福鑫已从年内收益 189% 跌⾄ 12% 。
当⻛险信号出现时，基⾦经理为何没能及时调整？经历回调之后的 AI 资产应如何定价？当前估值
回归合理区间了吗？ AI 之外，应该投什么？基⾦经理在三季报中给出了各⾃的思考。
AI 回调后的反思
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

三季度 AI 产业链持续回调，未能及时调整的基⾦经理经受了净值回撤。从基⾦三季报中可以看
到，基⾦经理们从不同维度进⾏了反思。
⾸先是⻛险识别能⼒不⾜。
⼤成基⾦郭玮羚在季报中反思道，三季度科技成⻓板块跌幅⼤，此前重仓 AI 科技硬件，在三季度
表现糟糕，主要是 AI 产业经过上半年的⾼速发展，预期较为乐观，忽视了发展过程中阶段性的潜
在⻛险。
南⽅基⾦章晖提及，基⾦未能及时降低通信和电⼦的仓位，导致跑输基准和同业。⻓安基⾦徐
⼩勇反思道： “ 我们对市场⻅顶认识不⾜，反应⾮常滞后，导致净值回撤巨⼤。 ”
部分基⾦经理对拥挤度、流动性等问题重视不够也导致了较⼤回撤。
中欧基⾦杜厚良表⽰， “ 过于看重中⻓期产业逻辑，对短期资⾦⾯冲击的评估仍有优化空间，⻛
险控制安排有待完善 ” ，⽽且港股配置⽐例偏⾼，所持港股多为中⼩市值科技硬件标的，交易流
动性较弱。市场波动加⼤放⼤了持仓的流动性折价，抬⾼了调仓冲击成本，拖累了组合表现，
也限制了应对空间。
中欧数字经济基⾦经理冯炉丹提及： “ 需要反思的是，我们在关注产业景⽓度和公司基本⾯的同

时，对于市场持仓结构、流动性和交易拥挤度由量变向质变演化的重视程度不够，未能较好地
应对三季度的较⼤幅度回撤。 ”
国泰多资产增益先锋基⾦经理刘健琦认为： “ 机构对科技持仓的集中度升⾄历史⾼位，拥挤度本
⾝构成脆弱性，后续需更加重视拥挤度信号，做好⻛险预算管理。 ” 易⽅达彭珂亦将本轮 AI 板块回
调的触发因素归为 “ 极致拥挤的筹码结构叠加局部估值泡沫化特征突出 ” 。
⼀部分反思指向了个⼈投资框架的缺陷。
富荣福银基⾦经理郭梁良反思道，原来的框架中，景⽓反转和兑现度下降是组合减仓的依据，
但三季度的⾏情中，⾏情影响因素更多作⽤于估值，⽽⾮盈利，这导致组合对此类⻛险应对不
够敏感。
华商基⾦⾼兵也在季报中再次反思道， “ 以前的产业趋势研究框架短周期内可能失效，⽆法更好
地应对这个市场。 ”
华泰紫⾦价值甄选基⾦经理查晓磊、袁作栋、华泰紫⾦丰睿基⾦经理吴晖的追问则回到收益归
属： “ 我们也在反思，哪些是市场的 Beta （市场系统性收益），⻛格的 Beta ，哪些是真实的 Alpha

（超额收益）。 ”
⼀些基⾦经理虽然进⾏了减仓，但低估了回调幅度。
“ 我们按照事先设定的条件降低了相关仓位，但减仓主要发⽣在下跌过程中，没有在⻛险释放之
前完成，这是本季度最需要反思的地⽅。 ” 国泰基⾦赵⼤震在三季报中写道， “ 复盘以后，我们进
⼀步认识到，持有多家公司并不意味着组合真正分散，如果这些公司的收⼊和盈利最终取决于
同⼀个客⼾群体、同⼀种资本开⽀或者同⼀个产业变量，组合本质上仍然⾼度集中。后续我们
会更加严格地控制单⼀产业和单⼀需求来源的⻛险暴露。 ”
“ 尽管我们在 7 ⽉已对部分涨幅较⼤的科技股进⾏减仓，但仍低估了市场下跌的幅度。 ” 陈聪在兴全
合熙中提及。⻓城基⾦储雯⽟总结道， “ 在⼤趋势向下时，科技板块内部的调仓⽆益于回撤的控

### Page 3

制。 ” 华泰柏瑞赵楠亦坦⾔，更偏向个股的基本⾯情况，未能够提前识别市场的⻛险，果断采取有
⼒的措施控制净值的⼤幅回撤。
AI 还值得坚守吗？
AI 算⼒相关股价下跌之后，部分基⾦经理给出了最直接的⼀类回答：产业趋势没变，股价跌下
来，正是选择⼊场的好机会。
景顺⻓城基⾦杨锐⽂的转变颇具代表性。⼆季度，他还对部分个股估值超出传统定价体系⼼存
顾虑，三季度则更为乐观。他提到，经过三季度调整，市场结构性泡沫明显收敛，很多被错
杀、估值回归的机会正在出现，适合主动加⼤⼒度挖掘低估机会。
然⽽，杨锐⽂并不认为 AI 会回到⻬涨共跌的状态。 “ 科技板块⻬涨共跌的强⻉塔时代已经结束，
接下来是典型的结构性个股⾏情。 ” 他在季报中写道，⽐拼的核⼼聚焦到了底层研究选股能⼒。上
⼀季度，他的组合中⾮ AI ⽅向持仓虽受市场⻛格偏好影响阶段性承压，但相关标的估值处于历史
极低分位，同时具备扎实优异的内⽣成⻓能⼒，因此选择继续坚定持有。
⻓期押注半导体的银华基⾦⽅建动作更为积极。他在季报中坦⾔，市场如期回调，但后续调整

幅度远超出预期；全球 AI 产业趋势依然明确，半导体国产化基本⾯正在加速兑现。操作层⾯，他
在季度初稍微降了⼀些仓位，持仓结构基本维持；到季度中下旬，随着市场调整到 “ 基本⾯合适
位置 ” 时，⼜将仓位加到基本满仓⽔平。四季度，他仍将重仓低估值、⾼成⻓、⼤空间和强确定
性的半导体国产化标的，同时关注受益于 AI 浪潮的芯⽚设计和制造公司。
财通基⾦⾦梓才认为，产业链上游供给紧张的格局仍将维持，只要 AI 的⾼增⻓需求持续存在，上
游产品价格短期内或仍难回落。资本市场或需要⼀些时间，才能逐步消化这些环节的产品价格
较难回落的现实。因此，组合仅对持仓个股进⾏微调，整体板块配置保持相对稳定。
从业绩看，⽅建和⾦梓才均在上半年完成净值翻倍，⼆季度管理规模急速扩张，但三季度经历
了净值⼤幅回撤。⽅建管理的银华集成电路年内峰值收益 144.75% ，当前回落⾄ 21.59% ，该产
品规模（ A 份额 +C 份额）⼀季度末不⾜ 80 亿元，⼆季度末快速扩张⾄ 278.5 亿元；⾦梓才管理的
财通多策略福鑫年内峰值收益 189.37% ，居全市场⾸位，当前回落⾄ 12.5% ，其管理规模由⼀季

度末的不⾜百亿元升⾄⼆季度末的 565.48 亿元，三季度缩⽔ 128.84 亿元。
AI 之外投什么？
三季度⼀些基⾦经理选择从 AI 撤离，⼜投向了哪些板块？
反思减仓不够早的赵⼤震，其管理的国泰⾦⿅混合三季度净值下跌 20.1% ，过去六个⽉增⻓
49.52% 。从持仓来看，⼆季度重仓股⼏乎全部来⾃光模块、 PCB 等板块，三季度则转向了医药
研发服务⾏业。
对于科技之外的再平衡选择，易⽅达彭珂认为 AI 产业的发展和内需市场的复苏是市场中两个最重
要的要素，也是组合配置最核⼼考虑因素。虽然核⼼内需公司估值和市场预期也处于较低状
态，但预计复苏仍将需要漫⻓时间和更多政策⽀撑。 “ 基于中期布局的思路，我们适当布局了⼀
些内需⻰头公司，同时也布局了  CXO （医药研发⽣产外包）、银⾏、红利等公司，使得整体组合
⻛险暴露相对更为均衡。 ”
“ 科技产业的发展从来不是⼀蹴⽽就，⽽股票市场预期却往往线性外推或更为乐观，产业趋势本
⾝的进程和股票市场预期的错位，将是产业趋势后期科技股投资⾯临的主要⽭盾。 ” 彭珂分析，在

科技形成新⼀轮更为清晰的产业趋势前，整体科技⾏情⼤幅缩圈或难以避免，仔细甄别估值业

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

图⽚
绩匹配度⾼的公司将更为重要，先不败⽽后求胜，尽量规避⼤的回撤，将是未来⼀段时间科技
投资思路的核⼼。
易⽅达杨宗昌在⾏业配置上，增加了 CXO 、煤炭、有⾊⾦属及机械设备⾏业的配置，组合⾏业
配置更为分散、均衡。他对⼈⼯智能产业发展的前景 “ 仍然保持乐观 ” ，同时也在加⼤对传统⾏业
优质公司的研究，认为短期逆⻛可能反⽽带来好的价格和买⼊时机。
⼴发基⾦冯汉杰三季度调仓频率较低，仅对部分涨跌幅较⼤的个股做了适度调整，并在煤炭⾏
业内部及下游⾏业之间进⾏结构性微调，组合仓位与结构整体变化不⼤。展望下⼀阶段，他仍
计划保持审慎，等待基本⾯出现显著变化或估值进⼊理想的低估区间。
冯汉杰认为，尽管较多 AI 相关股票已明显回调，但当前价格所隐含的基本⾯假设仍然偏于积极；

传统⾏业诸多板块虽出现明显上涨，也不⾜以⽀撑整体⼤幅上⾏的判断。他将近两三个季度的
波动归因于短期因素扰动，从⻓期价值看，股价整体处于相对合理区间，尚未出现系统性低估
机会。
鹏华基⾦陈⾦伟继续坚守内需赛道。他观察到，三季度随着科技板块下跌，此前受 AI 硬件资⾦虹
吸效应压制的其他板块出现⼩幅修复。然⽽这种修复整体并⾮由狭义基本⾯驱动，更多源于市
场对 AI 的⼀致预期有所松动。严格意义上，传统板块三季度较⼆季度仍略有⾛弱。他仍然看好内
需类资产，包括部分消费、医药和中游周期，在基本⾯已现拐点的⽅向中选股。
本⽂来源：财经杂志

7-9 月期ユーロ圏 GDP 減速しつつも上振れ 
実質 GDP 成長率は前期比＋0.4％と市場予想を上回る

ロンドンリサーチセンター シニアエコノミスト 橋本 政彦

[要約] 
 ユーロ圏の2026 年7-9 月期の実質GDP 成長率（速報値）は前期比＋0.4％と、4-6 月期
（同＋0.6％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（Bloomberg 調査：同＋0.2％）
を上回る底堅い結果であった。 
 ユーロ圏の成長率の半分は、多国籍大 企業の影響を受けたアイルランドの高成長によ
るものである点は割り引いて見る必要がある。 ただし、アイルランドを除いてもプラ
ス成長を維持しており、 エネルギー高を背景としたインフレ率の加速や、記録的猛暑
による悪影響という逆風にもかかわらず、ユーロ圏経済は底堅く推移したという評価
に変わりはない。 
 国別の内訳では、スペインが内需の堅調さを主因として前期比＋0.6％と高成長を維持
し、ユーロ圏全体をけん引する構図が続いた。また、ドイツは同＋0.2％と、前期から
成長率は鈍化したものの、市場予想（同＋0.1％）を上回った。他方、フランスは同▲
0.1％と 2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長に転じる低調な結果であった。 
 ユーロ圏経済は 10-12 月期も緩やかな景気拡大が続く見通しである。エネルギー価格
の上昇によるインフレ率の加速が個人消費を下振れさせるリスクには注意が必要だが、
ドイツを中心とした拡張財政がユーロ圏経済を下支えすると見込まれる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 6

成長の半分はアイルランドの上振れ、ただしそれを割り引いても底堅い 
ユーロ圏の 2026 年 7-9 月期の実質 GDP 成長率（速報値）は前期比＋0.4％と、4-6 月期（同
＋0.6％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（Bloomberg 調査、以下同：同＋0.2％）
を上回る底堅い結果であった。 
ただし、7-9 月期の上振れには、毎期の振れが非常に大きいアイルランドの高成長が影響し
ている点は割り引いて見る必要がある。 アイルランドの成長率は前期比 ＋5.5％と、成長率が
公表された 12 ヵ国の中で突出して高く、これがユーロ圏全体の成長 のおよそ半分を占めてい
る。CSO（アイルランド中央統計局 ）は、多国籍企業の影響が大きいセクターが押し上げ要因
になったと報告しており、一部の多国籍大企業の企業内取引などが成長率を押し上げたとみら
れる。アイルランドを除くユーロ圏 20 ヵ国の GDP 成長率（大和総研試算）は同＋0.2％となり、
実態としての景気回復ペースはヘッドラインよりも緩やかだったとみるべきだろう。もっとも、
アイルランドを除いてもプラス成長を維持しており、エネルギー高を背景としたインフレ率の
加速や、記録的猛暑による悪影響という逆風にもかかわらず、ユーロ圏経済は底堅く推移した
という評価に変わりはない。 
国ごとの成長率を確認 すると、スペイン、オランダ、エストニアがいずれも前期比＋ 0.6％
となり、既述のアイルランドの次に成長率が高かった。スペインの成長率は前期（同＋ 0.7％）
から減速したが、ユーロ圏全体に対する寄与度はアイルランドに次いで大きく、スペインの高
成長がユーロ圏全体をけん引する構図は7-9 月期も続いた。また、ドイツは同＋0.2％と、こち
らも前期（同＋0.3％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（同＋0.1％）を上回り、ユ
ーロ圏全体の成長を下支えした。他方、不調だったのがフランスであり、増加を見込んでいた
市場予想（同＋0.1％）に反して、同▲0.1％と2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長に転じている。

（注）左図の「その他」は、ユーロ圏全体から表示された5 ヵ国（ドイツ、フランス、イタリア、スペイン、ア
イルランド）を差し引いて計算。

フランスは輸出・投資が減少、スペインは引き続き内需主導の成長維持 
ユーロ圏全体の 7-9 月期の需要項目別の内訳は速報段階で明らかになっていないため、以下
では内訳が明らかになっているフランスとスペインの需要項目別の動きを見ていく。 
フランスの実質 GDP 成長率は前期比▲0.1％と、2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となった。内・
外需別では、内需の前期比寄与度は＋0.5％pt とプラスだった一方、外需寄与度が同▲0.6％pt
となり GDP を押し下げた。内需の内訳では、在庫変動が最大の押し上げ要因（前期比寄与度：
＋0.4％pt）となった。ただし、内需から在庫を除いた国内最終需要も 前期からわずかに増加
ペースが加速しており、GDP 全体としてはマイナス成長ながら、内需については一定の底堅さ
が見られたと評価できる。 
需要項目別の詳細を見ていくと、個人消費は前期比 ＋0.4％と前期から加速し、これが内需
を押し上げた。増加幅は2024 年7-9 月期以来の大きさであり、個人消費に限れば7-9 月期はむ
しろ好調だった と評価できる。内訳 では、財（同＋0.4％） 、サービス（同＋0.4％）がいずれ
も前期から加速しており、 個人消費は幅広く増加し ている。財消費では、エネルギーが同＋
1.2％と増加に転じたことが主な押し上げ 要因となった 。ガソリン価格 が上昇する中、石油精
製品の減少は続いたものの、電力消費の増加がエネルギー全体を押し上げた。また、エネルギ
ー以外の財では 、電気自動車の販売増加により、輸送用機器が同＋3.1 ％と好調だった。サー
ビス消費では、娯楽関連を中心とした家庭向けサービスの増加（同＋0.9％）、および宿泊・飲
食サービス（同＋0.7％）が主な押し上げ要因となった。 
好調だった個人消費とは対照的に、総固定資本形成 は前期比▲0.6％と 3 四半期連続で減少
し、内需の足を引っ張った 。最大の押し下げ要因となったのは、 輸送用機器が同▲7.0％と大
幅に減少したことである。統計公表元の INSEE（フランス国立統計経済研究所）の報告によれ
ば、前期にあったクルーズ船の受け渡しの反動、および企業による投資の減少が押し下げ 要因
となった。このほか、建設投資が同▲0.5％と3 四半期連続で減少したほか、不動産取引の停滞
により、法律サービスなどのビジネスサービス（同▲ 1.6％ ）、不動産サービス （同▲0.7％）
が前期から減少した。他方、IT 機器の増加により資本財が同＋0.8％と増加したほか、サービ
ス投資の中でも情報通信サービスは同＋2.1％と増加した。 
外需に関して、輸出は前期比▲1.7％と2 四半期ぶりに減少した。前期に大幅に増加した航空
関連が反動で 同▲9.1 ％と大きく減少したことが減少の主因と なった。 また、 農産品 （同▲
7.0％）、農作物加工品（同▲1.1％）が減少したほか、資本財が同▲1.1％と 7 四半期ぶりの減
少に転じるなど 、財輸出が総じて振るわなかった。 サービス輸出は同＋0.5％と増加を維持し
たが、増加幅は前期（同＋1.0％）から減速している。 
輸入は前期比＋0.1％と、小幅ながら増加した。自動車、および海軍装備品の増加によって
輸送用機器が同＋0.3％増加したことや、資本財の増加（同＋1.4％）が押し上げに寄与した。
他方、石油精製品（同▲6.4 ％ ）、農作物加工品（同▲1.6％）が減少したことで 、輸入全体の
増加幅は小幅にとどまった。

### Page 4

1

4 / 6

図表 2 フランス・スペインの実質 GDP 成長率と需要項目別寄与度

（出所）INSEE、INE、Haver Analytics より大和総研作成

スペインの実質 GDP 成長率は前期比＋0.6％となり、4-6 月期（同＋0.7％）から成長ペース
が減速しつつも、高い成長率を維持した。内 ・外需別では、内需 の前期比寄与度が ＋0.5％pt、
外需が同＋0.1％pt となり、引き続き堅調な内需が成長のドライバーとなっている。 
個人消費は前期比＋0.6％となった。形態別の内訳では、サービスが同＋0.9％と、15 四半期
連続で増加し好調さを維持したことに加え、耐久財が同＋0.5％と2 四半期連続で増加し、非耐
久財は同＋0.6％と 4 四半期ぶりの増加に転じている。他方、半耐久財は同▲2.2％と減少に転
じた。 
総固定資本形成は前期比＋0.7 ％と、11 四半期連続 の増加を維持した。ただし、増加幅は
2025 年 4-6 月期以来の小ささとなり、増加ペースは鈍化している。建設投資が同＋1.0％と堅
調さを維持する一方で、輸送用機械の減少が下押し要因となり、機械・設備・武器システムは
同＋0.2％と小幅な増加にとどまった。 
外需に関して、輸出は前期比＋1.3％と4 四半期連続で増加し、増加幅が前期から拡大した。
インバウンド（非居住者による支出）の増加（ 同＋1.6％）を中心に、サービス 輸出が同＋
1.5％と増加し、輸出全体をけん引する状況が続いている が、サービス輸出 の伸びは前期から
縮小した。他方、財輸出は同＋1.1％と、増加幅はサービス輸出に比べて小さいものの、前期
から増加ペースが加速している。 
輸入は前期比＋1.1％となった。サービス輸入が同＋2.1％と大きく増加したことに加え、財
輸入も同＋0.8％と増加した。輸入の増加幅は前期から縮小し、輸出の増加幅を下回ったため、
外需（純輸出）の寄与度は2 四半期ぶりのプラスに転じている。 
なお、ドイツ （前期比＋0.2 ％）については、まだ内訳の詳細な計数は公表されていないが、

5 / 6

ドイツ連邦統計局からは、消費支出の増加が押し上げに寄与したこと、一方で総固定資本形成
が減少したことが報告されている。また、輸出については好調だった2026 年上期から減速した
とコメントされた。イタリア（同横ばい）については、内需寄与度のプラスと、外需寄与度の
マイナスがイタリア国家統計局によって報告されている。これらの情報を総合すると、7-9 月
期は、個人消費を中心とした内需がユーロ圏の成長に寄与した可能性が高い。

防衛・インフラ投資が景気拡大を下支え、リスクはエネルギー価格 
ユーロ圏経済の 10-12 月期に入ってからの景気動向に関して、欧州委員会が公表するユーロ
圏景況感指数（総合）を確認すると、2026 年10 月は前月差▲0.6pt と2 ヵ月連続で低下した。
もっとも、過去の関係に照らせば10 月の指数の水準は、景気悪化を示すほどの低さではなく、
ユーロ圏経済は引き続き緩やかなペースでの拡大が続いていることを示唆する。 
景況感指数の内訳を見ると、消費者信頼感指数が前月差▲1.1pt と 2 ヵ月連続で低下したほ
か、これに平仄を合わせる形で小売業が同▲1.5pt と低下した。これは、エネルギー価格の上
昇によってインフレ率のさらなる加速に対する懸念が高まっていることや、生活不安を理由 と
した各地での抗議活動の広がりが影響したとみられる。実際、10 月に入って大規模なデモや暴
動が発生したフランスの消費者信頼感指数は、同▲3.4pt と主要国の中でも目立って大きく前
月から悪化した。サービス業の景況感は依然として高水準ながら、同▲0.6pt と 6 ヵ月ぶりの
低下に転じたのも、消費者マインドの悪化や、それを背景とした需要の減少が下押し要因とな
った可能性が高い。

図表 3 ユーロ圏の景況感指数と実質 GDP 成長率（左） 、景況感指数の内訳（右）

（出所）Eurostat、欧州委員会より大和総研作成

一方、家計部門の弱含みとは対照的に、鉱工業の景況感はこのところ堅調さを増している。
鉱工業の景況感指数は、前月差＋0.5pt と 5 ヵ月連続で改善し、2023 年 4 月以来の高さとなっ
た。鉱工業の景況感の回復には、拡張財政による防衛支出・インフラ投資関連の需要の増加が

寄与しているとみられる。足元では長期金利が上昇ペースを速めており、これが設備投資や公
共投資の拡大を抑制する恐れはあるものの、積極的に財政を拡張 させるドイツを中心とした防
衛支出、インフラ投資がユーロ圏経済を下支えする構図は当面続くと見込まれる。 
先行きのリスク要因としては、引き続きエネルギー価格の動向、およびその影響の広がりが
焦点となる。今回の 7-9 月期の GDP 統計では、インフレ率が加速する中でも個人消費が底堅く
推移したことが確認された。だが、原油やガスなどのエネルギー価格は上昇が続いており、実
質所得や消費者マインドへの下押し圧力は、今後さらに強まる可能性がある。とりわけ冬季の
需要期にはエネルギー価格上昇による負担感が一層強まるとみられ、個人消費の下振れに警戒
する必要があるだろう。

### Page 1

00

SK 하이닉스 (000660/KS) 
여전히 강력하다 
3Q26 영업이익 83 조원으로 예상 상회 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 83조원 (+38% QoQ)으로 예상을 상회했다. 메모
리 공급 부족 심화, HBM4 반영 시작에 따른 메모리 가격 상승이 환율 하락을 상쇄했
기 때문이다. 출하 DRAM +10%, NAND -1%, 가격 DRAM +28%, NAND 
+24%, 영업이익률 DRAM 82%, NAND 72%, HBM 판가는 HBM4 출하가 시작되
며 37% 수준 상승한 것으로 추정된다. 업황은 여전히 예상을 상회하고 있다.

4Q26 분기 영업이익 100 조원 시대 진입 전망 
4Q26 영업이익은 105조원 (+27% QoQ)으로 분기 100조원 시대에 진입할 것으로 
전망한다. 메모리 가격 강세가 지속되는 가운데, 1c 출하 가속화, M15X 램프업 본격화 
등으로 출하량 상승이 기존 예상을 상회할 것이다. 또한 HBM4의 Cross-over 시작 
및 수율 안정화로 HBM 가격, 수익성 제고 동반이 지속될 것이다. 출하는 DRAM 
+24%, NAND +4%, 가격은 DRAM +8%, NAND +7%를 예상한다.

2027 년 영업이익 488 조원 (+70% YoY)으로 상향조정 
SK하이닉스의 2027년 영업이익을 488조원 (+70% YoY)으로 12% 상향 조정한
다. 원/달러 환율 전망치를 기존 1,340원에서 1,315원으로 하향 조정했음에도, 공급 
부족 심화 속 2027년향 HBM 가격 인상 및 예상을 상회하는 수율, 1Q27 진행될 모바
일 DRAM 가격 인상 등을 반영했기 때문이다. LTA 가격 역시 개별 약정된 범위 내 
시장 상황을 반영할 수 있는 구조라는 점을 감안하면, 지속 상승할 것이라 전망한다.

높은 주주환원 의지 확인, 높아진 실적 가시성, 지나친 저평가 
투자의견 매수, 목표주가 4,000,000원을 유지한다. SK하이닉스는 LTA에 대해 기
존 5년에서 추가 연장에 대한 논의가 진행 중이며, 2029년까지 HBM, DRAM은 사실
상 완판, HBM 수요는 공급 가능 물량의 2 배를 상회, 2030년까지 메모리 수요 우위 
국면 전망을 밝혔다. AI 인프라 투자의 강력한 명분과 메모리 수요의 구조성, 잠재적으
로 매우 높은 LTA 위반 비용을 감안하면 실적 가시성과 안정성 제고가 동반되는 국면
이다. 100조원 수준의 주주환원은 2026년 예상 FCF의 66% 수준, 분기별 자사주 
매입, 소각 지속 계획을 통해 높은 주주환원 의지를 보여주고 있다. 실적 Duration 장
기화, 주주환원을 감안하면 12MF P/E 3.4X 수준의 현 주가는 현저한 저평가 국면이
다. 과도한 우려의 되돌림과 메모리 밸류에 대한 재논의 국면 진입을 전망한다.

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.

당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다.

### Page 1

현대차 (005380)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.16  
4분기 반등 기대

3Q26 Preview 
현대차는 3Q26E 매출액 42.4조원(-9.4% YoY, -13.9% QoQ), 영업이익 2.5조원(-
2.8% YoY, -13.5% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 각 부문
별 영업이익은 자동차 1.7조원(-2.0% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 4.8%), 금융 5,501억
원(-4.5% YoY, -27.5% QoQ, OPM 6.9%)으로 예상한다. 
생산 정상화 및 신차 확대를 통한 수익성 개선 기대 
현대차는 글로벌 도매 판매 91.8만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 90.9
만대(-9.6%)를 기록했다. 7~8월에 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생산 차
질이 발생했다. 파업 영향으로 HEV 판매 증가율이 1%에 그치면서, HEV 판매 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선 효과도 시장 기대에 미치지 못했다.  
4분기부터는 3분기 파업 영향으로 지연된 생산량 회복 및 신차 확대가 본격화되면서, 
전분기 대비 매출 및 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 동사는 보급형 전기차 아이오닉3의 튀
르키예 생산을 9월부터 시작했으며, 이를 통해 유럽 시장 판매량은 점차 회복될 전망
이다. 또한, 1) HMGMA를 통한 HEV 미국 현지 생산 확대, 2) 제네시스 HEV 모델 순
차적 출시를 통해 내년부터 북미를 중심으로 믹스 개선이 본격화될 전망이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 520,000원으로 하향 
2027년 영업이익은 11.2조원(+10.2%)를 기록할 것으로 예상된다. 원화 약세 지속을 
반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다. 다만, 현재 주가는 
12M Fwd P/E 기준 6.1배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 수준이다. 1) RMAC 
성과 구체화, 2) RA(로봇 생산법인) 착공 등 Physical AI 사업이 구체화될 경우 밸류에
이션 할증이 가능할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr

Document 258: [402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439.pdf
Report source: Hankyung

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대 
예정보다 빠른 SK 하이닉스 자사주 매입 
SK스퀘어의 자회사인 SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 20일부터 11월 19일까지 자사주 40
조원(2,407만주, 3.3%)을 매입 예정이다. 9월 29일까지 1,715만주, 예정 매입수량 
대비 71.3%를 매입 완료하였다. 매입 초기 매일 65만주 매입하다가 9월 10일부터는 
60만주를 매입하고 있는데, 이러한 추세가 유지된다면 10월 중순경 자사주 매입이 마
무리될 예정이다. 자사주 매입 후 소각 예정으로 소각이 완료되면 SK스퀘어의 SK하아
닉스에 대한 지분율은 20.0%에서 20.7%로 상승하게 된다.

자사주 매입 이후 추가적인 주주환원에 대한 기대 
SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 19일 정책기간(‘25년~’27년) 동안 발생하는 누적 FCF의 
50% 이상을 주주환원 하는 주주환원 정책을 발표한 바 있다. 26년 SK하이닉스의 예
상 FCF는 164조원(컨센서스 기준)으로 50%를 주주환원 할 경우 자사주 매입 금액
(40 조원)을 제외한 주주환원 재원은 42 조원이다. 잔여 주주환원 재원이 모두 배당으
로 지급된다면 SK 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 20.7%이므로 
SK스퀘어의 배당수입도 8.7조원에 달한다. 지난 3월 SK스퀘어는 ‘26년~’28년 경
상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 정책을 발표했다. 보수적으로 30%만 
가정하더라도 SK스퀘어의 배당재원은 2.6조원(현 시가총액 대비 1.8%)이다. SK스퀘
어는 연초 주주총회에서 배당가능이익 확보 차원에서 자본준비금 5 조 8,900억원을 
이익잉여금으로 전입한 바 있다. 이는 26년 재무제표가 확정되는 27년 3월 이후 배당
가능이익으로 편입되므로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 확대가 기대된다.

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 185 만원(유지) 
SK스퀘어의 순자산 가치 중 98% 이상이 SK하이닉스 지분가치이다. SK하이닉스는 
자사주 매입 이후 17.7% 주가가 상승했으나, SK 스퀘어 주가는 11.4% 상승했다. SK
하이닉스 자사주 매입·소각은 SK 스퀘어의 보유 지분가치 상승뿐 아니라 지분율도 상
승하기 때문에 자사주가 소각될 경우 SK스퀘어의 주가가 탄력적으로 움직일 가능성이 
높다. SK스퀘어 현재 NAV 대비 할인율은 43.7%, 12MF PBR 1.4배로 7월 이후 주
가 조정으로 밸류에이션 매력도 높아졌다. 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 185만원을 유지
한다.

2026-09-30

지주회사/통신서비스/ESG. 최관순 / ks1.choi@sks.co.kr / 3773-8812

Document 330: European bank M&A is back with a bang

Source PDF: all report pdf/European bank M&A is back with a bang.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

European bank M&A has seen a resurgence in recent years, and
2027 should stay busy. Domestic deals still dominate, while
cross-border consolidation is harder but is also picking up.
Regulation is becoming more supportive, but politics and
execution still matter. Expect more deals, but a transformation
of Europe's banking sector is still some way oﬀ
Jesse Norcross
UniCredit and Commerzbank are at the centre of Europe’s closely watched
push towards cross-border banking consolidation

European bank M&A is back
European bank M&A has seen a strong resurgence, with several
large-scale deals making headlines. UniCredit’s high-stakes
takeover of Commerzbank, once deemed very unlikely but now
moving closer to completion, could be Europe’s most signiﬁcant
cross-border transaction in years. Italy remains one of Europe’s
bank M&A hotbeds, with multiple banks taking part in the ongoing
consolidation wave. Other banks have also been active, and we

expect M&A to remain elevated in 2027.

### Page 2

Year-to-date M&A deal value has already surpassed the 2025 total,
reaching €53bn including the UniCredit-Commerzbank transaction
and based on deals valued at €100m or more. Furthermore, deals
pending or intended total €39bn, which includes the potential
takeover of Banca Monte dei Paschi by Intesa Sanpaolo, currently
valued at around €34bn. All in, this would bring deal value back to
levels not seen since the early 2010s.
European bank M&A volumes have seen a

resurgence
(€bn)

Source: Reﬁnitiv, ING
The rationale for European bank M&A has changed. After the
ﬁnancial crisis, deals were mostly about repair, followed by a wave
of deals involving stronger domestic banks buying weaker rivals.
Today, the backdrop has shifted – proﬁtability and capital levels are
stronger, making the obvious cost-eﬃciency story less compelling

on its own. Instead, banks are using M&A to add scale, deposits and
fee-generating diversiﬁcation, while the need to close the gap with
US rivals remains a key driver.
The industrial logic has also strengthened, as ﬁxed costs are rising
in areas such as technology, AI, compliance, as well as personnel.
Rising investment requirements mean that remaining subscale is
becoming more expensive, even for highly proﬁtable banks.

### Page 3

Domestic dominates, but cross-border
competes
UniCredit-Commerzbank remains the sector’s most closely
watched deal and could become the ﬁrst major cross-border
acquisition since the ﬁnancial crisis. UniCredit has slowly been
building its stake rather than a clean takeover and is now deemed
to hold eﬀective control, despite currently owning around 50% of
the shares. Furthermore, it is also not entirely a leap into an
unfamiliar market, as UniCredit already has a signiﬁcant banking

business in Germany through its subsidiary, HVB.
The deal has also faced considerable pushback as well as political
and stakeholder opposition, but is now looking increasingly likely to
proceed. It has highlighted the diﬃculty of pulling oﬀ a large-scale
cross-border deal for European banks. Yet it is now becoming the
clearest live test of whether Europe is genuinely ready for cross-
border bank consolidation.
Domestic deals remain the rule, but we have seen other examples

of cross-border deals. These include BPCE’s acquisition of
Portuguese peer Novo Banco; Erste Bank's acquisition of Santander
Bank Polska; and Crédit Agricole acquiring IKB to increase its
presence in Germany. Some banks are also looking across the
pond, such as Santander, which acquired Webster Financial
Corporation in the US for USD12.5bn.
These transactions are important, but they do not yet amount to a
broad-based cross-border consolidation wave. Many involve banks

already familiar with the target market or assets that complement
an existing local business.
Selected transaction details

### Page 4

Italy is still riding the wave
Italy has become one of Europe’s busiest M&A markets, riding a
new wave of consolidation, with Banca Monte dei Paschi (MPS) at
the epicentre. The bank completed the acquisition of Mediobanca in
2025, and this year approached Banco BPM and Banca Generali for
a merger as a counteroﬀensive to Intesa Sanpaolo’s hostile bid for
MPS.
The counteroﬀensive now appears less likely, as key shareholders

have opposed parts of MPS’s merger plans and have also backed
Intesa’s bid. The ECB has also provided authorisation to take control
of MPS, representing an important step for the €34bn takeover.
Alongside this, we had the BPER–Popolare di Sondrio merger earlier
in the year, adding to Italy’s consolidation.
Why is Italy so active in particular? Well, Italy remains quite
fragmented (see below) and still oﬀers a large domestic synergy
pool, even after the wave of M&A that has already taken place.

Furthermore, the country has meaningful industrial scale and oﬀers
further revenue diversiﬁcation opportunities for banks and insurers.
Although the obvious combinations are becoming less evident in
Italy, we expect this to continue to drive M&A activity in 2027.

country (2025)

It's not only about getting bigger
The deal mix is also broadening, as banks are increasingly focusing
on fee income as well as scale, while also stepping into capital-light
businesses. Deals in sectors such as wealth management, asset
management, payments & FX and insurance are likely to feature
prominently.
NatWest Group’s acquisition of Evelyn Partners is a good example.

The combination created the UK’s largest Private Banking and
Wealth Management business, while lifting Natwest’s fee income
by 20%. Similarly, BNP Paribas’s acquisition of AXA Investment
Managers in 2025 created a €1.5tn asset management business,
as the bank is seeking to compete with US asset management
rivals and grow its fee income. In Germany, Deutsche Bank and
DWS were reported to be exploring a potential minority investment
in the life insurance business Frankfurter Leben. This would mark

Deutsche’s return to life insurance after more than a decade.
Consolidation can support bank
performance
But does consolidation actually deliver better outcomes? The
evidence is not entirely clear-cut, but overall points in the right
direction. While increasing scale is not always the best use of
capital, in general, more consolidated markets tend to have more
stable and, in some cases, higher proﬁtability. The ECB is in favour
of further consolidation where appropriate, as it sees the beneﬁts of

### Page 6

diversiﬁcation and eﬃciency. On the other hand, capital can be
reduced following a merger. This could be a downside, as better
capitalised banks tend to enjoy lower funding costs and thus have
more room to lend to the real economy.
The outcome therefore depends on the trade-oﬀ between longer-
term synergies and the upfront capital, execution and integration
costs.
ECB data show an association between more concentrated banking
markets and proﬁtability. As the chart below shows, countries with

a high level of concentration (deﬁned by a higher Herﬁndahl-
Hirschman Index coeﬃcient) tend to have higher net interest
margins on average. Still, net interest margins in Europe are
structurally lower than in the US, where they tend to be between 3–
4%.

### Page 7

Similarly, a higher level of market concentration in European
countries is associated with a lower cost of risk. The correlation is
modest, with some clear outliers, but the numbers show that
greater concentration can lead to lower asset quality costs,
although the relationship should perhaps not be interpreted as
causal. Still, taken together, this suggests that consolidation can
improve banking markets.

Regulation is helping, but can get in the way
The Banking Union has made deals easier to assess: the ECB has
become the single prudential supervisor for large banks, capital
rules are more consistent, while resolution planning is now much

clearer than it was even a decade ago. But as the Draghi report
noted, the incomplete Banking Union means Europe’s banks remain
less proﬁtable than their US counterparts.
There is a shift underway, though. The EU Commission plans to
table a broad Banking Package in the ﬁrst quarter of 2027, with
measures covering cross-border capital and liquidity management,
deposit insurance, prudential rules and other items. For M&A, the
most important proposals include those to give banking groups

more ﬂexibility to allocate capital and liquidity more eﬃciently
within cross-border groups, while also applying more consistent

### Page 8

treatment to intra-group exposures. These remain proposals rather
than agreed implementations, meaning their ﬁnal scope and
timetable remain uncertain.
The AFME estimates that regulatory ring-fencing rules mean that
some €225bn of capital and €250bn of liquidity is trapped within
subsidiaries, which banks cannot freely move between parent and
subsidiaries. Freeing this up could also support more cross-border
transactions.
Regulatory ring-fencing traps subsidiaries'

capital and liquidity
€225bn
€250bn
Capital Liquidity
Source: ECB, ING
The European Commission is also bringing forward a new proposal
to replace the European Deposit Insurance Scheme (EDIS), which
could reduce national ring-fencing incentives and encourage more
cross-border M&A. CMDI reform has been passed this year, which
could make it easier to deal with failing banks, potentially making
consolidation easier as well, although the direct impact on M&A is

unclear.
The Danish compromise, which is now embedded into CRR3, allows
banks to risk-weight certain insurance subsidiaries rather than
deduct them from regulatory capital. This was clariﬁed by the EBA
in January 2026, and some banks such as UniCredit have received
authorisation to use the Danish Compromise methodology for its
insurance subsidiaries. This has improved the economics of

### Page 9

bancassurance and could support further bank-insurance
transactions.
On the other hand, national regulatory and political intervention
has also put the brakes on M&A in some cases, raising execution
costs and uncertainty. BBVA’s proposed acquisition of Sabadell in
2024 illustrates the problem. It took 539 days from the
announcement in May 2024 to the oﬀer failing in October 2025.
For us, the message seems clear that European regulators are
seeking to improve bank competitiveness, and they see

consolidation as an important part of this. Looking ahead,
regulation can improve the plumbing, but it is not a dealmaker.
Why 2027 should stay busy
The starting point for 2027 remains supportive, with bank
proﬁtability continuing to hold up well in 2026 while capital levels
remain solid. While the geopolitical, inﬂation and interest rate
backdrop poses headwinds, we still expect acquisition activity to
remain high. On the other hand, management teams will

increasingly need to choose between retaining greater capital
headroom in a less market-friendly environment, shareholder
payouts, or M&A.
The most likely deals are still domestic. Italy remains the M&A
market of choice, but other geographies could also stand out. The
Nordics are likely to see more partnerships and tech- and wealth-
focused deals. Spain and Portugal are already quite concentrated,
so we could see more bolt-ons. In the UK, challenger banks and

neobanks are gaining ground while deposit competition remains
intense, potentially leading to more activity. The Benelux has
potential catalysts following a series of government ownership
reductions and increasing inorganic ambitions.
Cross-border activity could also remain elevated, but we think the
real litmus test remains the UniCredit-Commerzbank deal, including
what happens after completion in terms of execution and
integration, a process that is likely to take a few years. The

This publication has been prepared by ING solely for information purposes
irrespective of a particular user's means, ﬁnancial situation or investment

Read more
Download
Included in the following bundle
regulatory environment should also become increasingly more

supportive for cross-border M&A from 2027 onwards.
Overall, 2027 could be another interesting year.
M&A is here to stay
European bank M&A has picked up in recent years, and we expect
more activity again in 2027, although we do not see a big bang. For
investors, positioning is inherently speculative. For bondholders, the
main way to gain is by owning the target entity's bonds. However,
this is not always the case, and bigger doesn’t always mean better,

as credit ratings don’t necessarily improve either following an
acquisition or merger.
We are not suggesting that a major change in the European
banking landscape is imminent, as many obstacles remain,
particularly with cross-border deals. But the direction of travel is
interesting: larger banks often outperform smaller banks, while
regulators continue to see beneﬁts from a deeper, more integrated
European banking union. Still, European banking markets remain

fragmented to diﬀering degrees, both between countries and
compared to the US, presenting both opportunities and obstacles.

### Page 1

INFLATION TRACKER:
ENERGY PUSHES HEADLINE INFLATION HIGHER, CORE MEASURES
ARE STILL CONTAINED
October 2026

### Page 2

Key takeaways
2ECONOMIC RESEARCH
1 A rise in inflation still confined to energy
Rising energy prices are pushing
inflation higher in Europe and the
United States, while energy’s
contribution is slightly negative in
Japan.
3 Mounting pressure on producer prices
2 Some recent pressure on core measures
4 Rising short-termmarket expectations
Markets (swaps) are pricing this
rise in inflation into short-term
measures. Long-term expectations
(5y5y) are rising in Japan and the

UK.
Over recent months (3m/3m), core
indices have been firming in the
euro area and Japan: a signal to
watch.
In Europe and Japan, pressure on
producer prices is building. The
risk of the energy shock spilling
over to other CPI items is
increasing.

Style Exposure
We are incrementally positive on Jentech with multiple catalysts ahead. Near-term,

we believe the company’s 3Q26 earnings could be the first driver with a potential
GM beat, which should be reported in the second week of Nov. Longer-term, we
believe the drivers are from the incremental adoption of the removable lid in some
Nvidia Rubin GPUs in 2027 (in addition to Rubin Ultra), followed by the
meaningful Google TPU v9 spec upgrade potential in 2028 as another multi-fold
content increase story (based on JPMe), along with a structural margin expansion

trend given continued GPU and ASIC upgrades. Thus, we revise up our 2027E and
2028E EPS materially and are now 28% and 18% above the current BBGe
consensus.
• 3Q26 preview: expecting a stronger GM growth; 4Q26 outlook: slightly
better rev growth. We revise up our 3Q26 GM to 50.1% (vs current BBGe
consensus at 48.7%), which implies another quarter of strong GM expansion
(vs 2Q/1Q26 GM at 46.8%/41.8%), driven by better product mix to more AI
server business. Reported 3Q26 revenue of NT$9.5bn was largely in line with

the company’s guidance, and the 30% QoQ growth was mainly driven by the
decent ramp of Rubin GPU. As the Rubin 1-piece lid shipment should remain
at high volume into 4Q26, we believe the company’s revenue momentum will
also continue, with incremental growth from small volume of removable lid
shipment. Hence, we are now modeling Jentech’s 4Q26 revenue to grow 3%
QoQ, with GM further expanding thanks to ongoing mix enhancement.
• Further 2027 upside from incremental Rubin removable lid adoption;

2028 another earnings level-up year driven by meaningful upgrade in
TPU v9.We revise up our 2027E and 2028E revenue estimates materially by
22% and 14%, which are now 24% and 16% above consensus, respectively. For
the 2027 upward revision, it mainly reflects the incremental adoption of
removable lid (3-piece lid) in partial Rubin GPU from the next 1-2 quarter, in
addition to Rubin Ultra, as long as the company can ramp up its yield and
production of removable lid in the next few months with on-schedule capacity

expansion (new Taoyuan Aerotropolis plant) and equipment move-in (e.g. the
in-house screw locking equipment). More encouragingly, we believe 2028 will
see even greater earnings upside with Google TPU v9 likely upgrading its heat
spreader solution significantly to 2-piece or 3-piece lid design, in our view, as
the potential chip size (4-die) and TDP (potentially 2k+W or even higher)
should be a big level-up compared to v8, which makes the current stiffener-

only solution insufficient. As the content of 2-piece or 3-piece lid solution
could be 5-10x higher than the v8 stiffener, Google TPU v9 (with meaningful
volume) could drive the next wave of the earnings upgrade cycle for Jentech
from 2028 (following Nvidia Rubin/Rubin Ultra as the major driver in 2027).
• Implications. We lift our 2027E and 2028E EPS by 28% and 17% to NT$193
and NT$295, respectively, reflecting the incremental removable lid adoption

Sources for: Style Exposure – J.P. Morgan Global Markets Strategy; all other tables are company data and J.P. Morgan estimates.

Summary Investment Thesis and Valuation
Investment Thesis
Jentech (OW) is the largest packaging-level cooling component
maker worldwide, with leading production capabilities across
heat spreader products (including stiffener and all kinds of lids).
We expect the company to show a ~90% EPS CAGR over 2024-
28, driven by solid heat spreader content growth in future-gen AI

chips and increasing TAM into new solutions (e.g., CPO switch
ASIC). In addition, the company is reinforcing its product
portfolio with new offerings in cold plate and CPU server
sockets for chip customers. We believe Jentech’s technology
leadership in the structural cooling upgrade trend and its new
TAM expansion will continue to support its superior earnings
power in the long term.
Valuation
Our Dec-27 PT of NT$8,800 is based on 30x 2028E P/E, which

is largely in line with Jentech’s three-year average P/E when the
AI server boom started.

Investment Thesis, Valuation and Risks
Jentech Precision Industrial Co. (Overweight; Price Target: NT
$8,800.00)
Investment Thesis
Jentech (OW) is the largest packaging-level cooling component maker worldwide, with
leading production capabilities across heat spreader products (including stiffener and all
kinds of lids). We expect the company to show a ~90% EPS CAGR over 2024-28, driven

by solid heat spreader content growth in future-gen AI chips and increasing TAM into new
solutions (e.g. CPO switch ASIC). In addition, the company is reinforcing its product
portfolio with new offerings in cold plate and CPU server sockets for chip customers. We
believe Jentech’s technology leadership in the structural cooling upgrade trend and its new
TAM expansion will continue to support its superior earnings power in the long term.
Valuation

Our Dec-27 PT of NT$8,800 is based on 30x 2028E P/E, which is largely in line with
Jentech’s three-year average P/E when the AI server boom started.
Risks to Rating and Price Target
Downside risks to our rating and price target include: 1) Potential delay in mainstream 3-
piece/removable lid design adoption, and 2) market share loss from competition.

Economy 
Kicking and screaming: Child care costs persist 
20 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Child care remains a growing financial burden for many households, even as growth in the median payment has moderated, 
according to Bank of America payments data. More families are paying more than $2,000 per month for care, suggesting 
affordability pressures are becoming increasingly concentrated among households facing the highest expenses. 
• Child care cost pressures vary significantly across the country. Nashville and Jacksonville, FL posted the fastest year-over-year 
growth among major metro areas, while New York and Washington DC saw the biggest decrease in median payment growth, 
according to Bank of America payments data. 
• Limited supply may continue to constrain access to child care for some households. Child care employment fell 0.7% year-over-
year in September, suggesting provider capacity remains under pressure and could continue to affect household finances and 
labor force participation.

Child care costs outpace rent and medical services 
Child care remains a significant expense for many families. In September, the growth in child care and other school-related fees 
surpassed other major household expenses such as rent and medical services, according to the Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) 
(Exhibit 1). 
Yet, while child care prices continue to rise in official inflation data, median household payments have largely moderated since 
February, according to Bank of America data (Exhibit 2). More importantly, the growth in the number of households paying for 
child care has fallen since the start of 2025.

rent in the consumer price index

changing the amount of care they use, which is reflected in slower growth in the number paying for child care. As more

The decline in the number of households paying for child care may also reflect families seeking alternatives to formal care 
arrangements. Some may be relying more heavily on relatives, shifting parents’ work schedules or dropping out of the workforce 
altogether to care for their children in response to elevated costs. This could explain why, among all income groups paying for 
care, the number of lower-income households has seen the biggest decline, falling 2.0% year-over-year (YoY) in September 
(Exhibit 3). 
Exhibit 3: The number of lower-income households paying for child care has fallen the most, down 2.0% YoY in September

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
More families face larger monthly bills 
Yet even as fewer households pay for child care, the share of those shelling out more than $2,000 per month has increased 
substantially (Exhibit 4). In fact, it has more than doubled since 2023, reaching 7.7% in 2026 year-to-date. Meanwhile, the share 
of those paying $1,000 or less has declined, suggesting higher monthly payments are becoming more common even as the 
national median grows more slowly. 
Exhibit 4: The share of households paying >$2000 a month for child care has more than doubled since 2023 
Monthly child care payments by amount (annual average, share of households, %)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Rising costs take a bigger bite out of households’ paychecks 
What makes child care “affordable”? According to the U.S. Department of Health and Human Services, child care is considered 
affordable if it accounts for no more than 7% of family income. Yet, in Bank of America data, the average child care cost was 8% 
in September.  
While an 8% share may not appear dramatically above the federal affordability benchmark of 7%, it represents an average across 
households’ income levels. For families in high-cost metro areas or those paying more than $2,000 per month, the burden is 
likely considerably higher.

Where child care costs are climbing 
Child care pressures also differ substantially by location. In Bank of America payments data, Nashville posted the fastest YoY 
median payment growth in September, followed by Jacksonville, FL and Phoenix (Exhibit 6). This dispersion of growth across the 
country suggests child care cost pressures are no longer confined to historically expensive coastal markets.  
Interestingly, New York and Washington DC saw the sharpest decline in growth this year. Why might this be? The supply side of 
the market may help explain why – for example, New York City recently introduced universal Pre-K.1

Fewer providers, higher costs 
As of September 2026, employment in child care services was down 0.7% from a year earlier, suggesting the sector’s workforce 
recovery has stalled. Fewer workers can constrain the number of available places or the hours providers offer. That may leave 
families facing longer searches, fewer workable arrangements or continued price pressure. In turn, access to care can influence 
parents’ ability to enter, remain in or expand their participation in the workforce. 
Exhibit 7: Employment within child care services fell 0.7% YoY in September 
Employees by sector (monthly, seasonally adjusted, YoY%)

Source: Haver Analytics  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
What are the economic implications of rising child care costs? 
Child care costs may have important labor market implications for families with young children. Of those households paying for 
child care in 2026, more than half of them were Older Millennials (52%), followed by Younger Millennials (20%) and Gen X (16%), 
according to Bank of America payments data. This means child care costs weigh more heavily on Older Millennials. 
The share of Older Millennial households receiving more than one paycheck has continued to grow from a year earlier, but the 
pace of that improvement has moderated over the past three months (Exhibit 8). Given that this group accounts for the majority 
of households paying for child care in Bank of America payments data, they may offer early signs of how affordability affects 
their employment decisions. 
Exhibit 8: The share of Older Millennial households paying for child care that have more than one person with a paycheck has declined since 2021  
Share of households with a child care payment with more than one person with a paycheck (%, monthly, three-month moving average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Child care challenges can weigh on women’s employment 
According to data from the American Time Use Survey, women continue to perform significantly more child care and household 
work than men, even as their participation in paid work has increased. This suggests that persistent gaps in access and 
affordability may have disproportionate consequences for women’s employment. Indeed, the percentage of women who cited 
child care as a reason for temporary unemployment was nearly three times higher than men’s in 2026, though the average fell 
from 2025, according to data from the BLS. 
Yet labor force participation data indicates a more nuanced picture. Despite persistent child care pressures, mothers are 
participating in the workforce at rates that remain above their 2019 averages.

Methodology 
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Bank of America credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card holders 
making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards is excluded. Data regarding 
merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial services 
companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System (NAICS), 
which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
If applicable, the consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level.  
If applicable, any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a 
deposit account or credit card. Any reference to aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-
peer, cash and checks.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

Document 420: Top of Mind - Assessing a Less Transparent Fed

Source PDF: all report pdf/Top of Mind - Assessing a Less Transparent Fed.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs (Top of Mind)
Date: 2026-08-25
Pages: 26
Status: ok

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh is steering the Fed into a less transparent era. So, is that
a positive or negative development, and what could it mean for markets and the
economy? We speak with Former Fed governors Donald Kohn and Stephen Miran
as well as GS’ Jan Hatzius who agree that rigid “Odyssean” forward guidance that
commits policymakers to a pre-set path risks policy mistakes and is therefore rarely
appropriate. But they somewhat disagree about the benefits of great er transparency

around the Fed’s reaction function. Hatzius and Kohn argue that such transparency
helps markets better anticipate and price in policy changes, accelerating monetary
policy transmission. But Miran believes that the line between committing to a path
and being transparent is clearer in theory than in practice. And while GS economists
and strategists find that reduc ed transparency leads to increased market volatility,
the question is whether this just increases the market’s noise or also improves the

market’s signal. Hatzius argues largely for the former and Miran for the latter.
Jan Hatzius, Chief Economist and Head of Global Investment Research, Goldman Sachs
“Markets price what they think the Fed will do, not what they think the Fed should do. That will remain true even if the Fed
obscures its reaction function… market guesses about the Fed’s next moves will simply become worse, leading to more
volatility... and importantly, volatility that serves no constructive economic purpose.”

Donald Kohn, former Vice Chairman of the Board of Governors of the Federal Reserve System, Robert V . Roosa
Chair in International Economics and Senior Fellow, Brookings Institution
“I sympathize with Chairman Warsh to some extent. [But] the real problem with Warsh’s approach is his reluctance to articulate
how the FOMC sees the economy evolving… explaining the Fed’s thinking about the economic outlook is essential in helping
markets act as a stabilizing force and is a core component of accountability.”

We provide a brief snapshot on the most important economies for the global markets

### Page 3

El
Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research 3 
Top of Mind Issue 151

New Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh is steering the Fed into a less 
transparent era characterized by shorter post-meeting 
statements, less forward guidance, and a more limited role for 
projections, marking a meaningful break from the 
transparency revolution that has defined central banking over 
the last quarter century. So, what could a less transparent Fed 
mean for markets and the economy? 
For answers, we turn to former Fed governors Donald Kohn, 
now Robert V. Roosa Chair in International Economics and 
senior fellow at the Brookings Institution, and Stephen Miran, 
now senior strategist at Hudson Bay Capital Management, as 
well as Jan Hatzius, GS’ Chief Economist and Head of Global 
Investment Research. 
We first ask whether scaling back Fed communication is a 
positive or negative development. Hatzius says it’s likely 
negative, though he draws a clear line between transparency 
and forward guidance. He argues that clarity about the Fed’s 
reaction function helps markets better anticipate policy 
changes and quickly incorporate new information into financial 
conditions, thereby accelerating monetary policy transmission 
to the economy. So, he believes that unwinding that 
transparency would be a mistake. (Note: GS Global 
Economists Joseph Briggs and Megan Peters lay out the 
evidence backing this argument on pgs. 14-15.) 
But Hatzius takes a more nuanced view of forward guidance, 
arguing that rigid “Odyssean” guidance that commits 
policymakers to a pre-set path regardless of new information 
only makes sense at the effective lower bound for the policy 
rate, while conditional “Delphic” guidance tied to the 
economic outlook is generally more defensible. Although 
markets could confuse the two, he believes that most 
sophisticated investors understand the difference and is 
therefore not particularly worried that markets have become 
overly reliant on the policy dots published in the Fed’s 
Summary of Economic Projections (SEP). Instead, he 
considers them a “useful source of additional information 
about the Fed’s reaction function” (except for the long-run 
policy dot). If anything, Hatzius would prefer the dots move 
in an even more informative direction—by publishing each 
policymaker’s combined projections for growth, inflation, 
unemployment, and the policy rate on an anonymized basis. 
Kohn agrees that clarity about the Fed’s “narrative” and 
reaction function is vital. Explaining how the Fed sees the 
economy evolving and how policy would respond, he says, is 
“essential in helping markets act as a stabilizing force and is 
a core component of accountability.” Still, he sympathizes 
somewhat with Chairman Warsh’s view that the dot plot is 
“not helpful” (see pg. 9 for Warsh’s own words on this), 
arguing that the market’s hyper-focus on the SEP’s median 
policy dot “illustrates the risk of excessive precision and 
false certainty,” as small shifts in it can trigger unwarranted 
market moves. So, he has long favored omitting the median. 
Miran, though, is more supportive of Warsh’s push for a less 
transparent Fed. Like Hatzius and Kohn, he’s concerned that 
excessive forward guidance that locks the Fed into a policy 
path raises the risk of policy errors and also argues that such 
guidance can impair market pricing of risk. But he’s less 
convinced about the benefits of increased transparency 
around the Fed’s reaction function, making the case that the 
distinction between the Fed committing to a pre-set policy 
path and being transparent about its reaction function is clear 
in theory, but less so in practice. So, he thinks “the policy dots 
absolutely have to go” but feels less strongly about omitting 
the SEP’s economic projections. 
But would less transparency allow markets to provide the Fed 
with more “direct and unfiltered” information about the 
economy, as Warsh has argued? The answer seems to boil 
down to the implications for market volatility, and whether any 
resulting volatility would simply increase the market’s noise or 
also improve its signal. 
As for the volatility implications, the evidence is fairly clear. 
Briggs and Peters find that increased central bank 
transparency has led to a reduction in market volatility over the 
last several decades. This is consistent with GS Head of US 
Rates Strategy William Marshall’s recent observation of the 
reverse: that the US rates market has already priced 
substantial uncertainty associated with a less transparent Fed, 
with the July FOMC representing the largest non-cut meeting 
day surprise in almost three decades.  
But Marshall argues that whether such meeting-day surprises 
lead to structurally higher rates volatility depends (again) on 
whether the Fed’s reaction function is well understood. If it’s 
not, he would expect higher rates volatility to persist. GS FX 
Strategists Michael Cahill and Lexi Kanter find a similar 
pattern in FX: that central bank communication shifts can 
change which events and data drive FX volatility without 
necessarily raising it, but less transparency could increase 
Dollar volatility—all else equal (though global macro 
conditions remain its biggest driver).  
But the key debate seems to be whether the increased 
volatility resulting from less Fed transparency will improve or 
worsen the market’s signal. Hatzius is clearly in the “worsen” 
camp. He argues that markets price what they think the Fed 
will do—not what it should do—and less transparency won’t 
change that; it would simply produce worse guesses. So, he 
says that while volatility in and of itself is not necessarily bad, 
volatility driven by uncertainty about the Fed’s reaction 
function would serve no constructive economic purpose. 
Kohn agrees that the lack of a clear Fed narrative could create 
damaging uncertainty. But he argues that removing overly 
specific guidance could help free markets to process new 
information more independently and isn’t too worried about 
modestly higher volatility as a result. And Miran seems most 
convinced that reducing transparency would improve the 
markets’ signal, with volatility an unavoidable but acceptable 
cost: “The first best world of signal without volatility doesn't 
exist. I'd rather have that signal, even if it's messy, than 
throw the signal out by anchoring market expectations to the 
Fed's near-term projections.”
